\delta\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}

(Mixed System)

\frac{}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{id}}{},}\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{sub}}{},}\frac{}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathsf{i}:\mathsf{I}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_{I}$}{},}\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathsf{I}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{i}\,=\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_{E}$}{},}\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}(\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\otimes_{I}$}{},}\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{C}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathsf{let}\,(\mathit{x},\mathit{y})=\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\otimes_{E}$}{},}\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma,\mathit{A})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{\delta\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\multimap_{I}$}{},}\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\multimap_{E}$}{},}

\delta\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}

(Mixed System (continued))

\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GT}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}}{\mathit{r}*\delta\odot\Delta;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{t}:\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Grd}_{I}$}{},}\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GT}}\mathit{t}:\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}{\delta\odot\Delta;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathsf{Unlin}\,\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Lin}_{E}$}{},}\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{x}=\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Grd}_{E}$}{},}\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{weak}}{},}\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}[\mathit{x}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{cont}}{},}\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{ex}}{},}\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MT}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{gex}}{},}

Appendix C
Proofs of Properties of
\mathsf{mGL}{}

C.1
Cut Reduction of
\mathsf{mGL}{}

Cut reduction done termless for simplicity.

Lemma 23

(Cut Reduction GS)
.

If
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is a proof of
\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is a proof of
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

and
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
,
then there exists a proof
\Pi
of the sequent
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
.

Proof.

This is by induction on
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.

1.

Commuting Conversions

(a)

left-side:
Suppose we have

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{m}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{m}}]),(\delta\boxast[{(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{m}}]),(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})}^{\mathit{m}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

and
(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\gamma
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]),(\delta\boxast[{\gamma}^{\mathit{n}}]),(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\gamma\\
(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])(\mathit{j})=\gamma(\mathit{j})\text{ for any }j,1<=j<=n\\
\delta(\mathit{k})=\delta(\mathit{k})\text{ for any }k,1<=k<=m\end{array}

From this and the monotonicity properties of the operators in the resource
algebra it follows that:

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\bigoplus^{\mathit{m}}_{\mathit{k}=1}(\delta(\mathit{k})*(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])))=(\bigoplus^{\mathit{m}}_{\mathit{k}=1}(\delta(\mathit{k})*\gamma(\mathit{j})))\\
\delta\boxast[{(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])}^{\mathit{m}}]=\delta\boxast[{\gamma}^{\mathit{m}}]\\
((\delta\boxast[{(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{m}}])\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta\boxast[{\gamma}^{\mathit{m}}])\end{array}

and

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\end{array}

This implies:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

Thus, we obtain our result

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

(b)

cut vs. right-side cut (left case):
Suppose we have:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{m}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},(\delta^{\prime}\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}}^{\mathit{m}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{m}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

and
(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\gamma
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},(\delta^{\prime}\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}}^{\mathit{m}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\gamma\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\end{array}

This implies:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

Thus, we obtain our result:

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

(c)

cut vs. right-side cut (right case):
Suppose we have:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the previous case.

2.

\eta
-Expansions

(a)

Tensor:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

Since
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,0
we find ourselves in a base
case.
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
are cut-free, so we must produce a
cut-free proof,
\Pi
with
1\boxast[{1}^{\mathit{n}}]=1
,
which we do as follows:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{Y}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,}}{(1,1)\odot(\mathit{X},\mathit{Y})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$,}}{1\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,}

(b)

Tensor Unit:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathsf{J}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathsf{J}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

Since
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,0
we find ourselves in a base
case.
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
are cut-free, so we must produce a
cut-free proof,
\Pi
with
1\boxast[{1}^{\mathit{n}}]=1
,
which we do as follows:

\frac{\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_R${}$,}}{1\odot\mathsf{J}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$,}

(c)

Lin:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

Since
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,0
we find ourselves in a base
case.
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
are cut-free, so we must produce a
cut-free proof,
\Pi
with
1\boxast[{1}^{\mathit{n}}]=1
,
which we do as follows:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$,}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_R${}$,}

3.

Axiom Cases

(a)

Axiom on the left:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

We know:

\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is a proof of
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}

and that
\mathit{r}*1=\mathit{r}

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

(b)

Axiom on the right:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}\end{array}

We know:

\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is a proof of
\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}

and that
1\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

4.

Principal Formula vs Principal Formula

(a)

Tensor:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\frac{\,}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}))\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathit{r}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})=(\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

Instead of applying the induction hypothesis,
we can directly build the proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}\quad\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi_{5}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

So
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})

(b)

Tensor Unit:

\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{J})\\
\mathit{r}*\emptyset=\emptyset\end{array}

So
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
is a proof of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\emptyset,\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\emptyset,\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{J})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

5.

Secondary Conclusion

(a)

Left Introduction of Tensor:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Z}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Z})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{W}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Z})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Z})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

Implies

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

(b)

Left Introduction of Tensor Unit:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})

where
|\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}|
and
|\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}|
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},(\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}]),\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}]\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

(c)

Weakening:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{weak${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})

where
|\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}|
and
|\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}|
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{weak${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\mathsf{0}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\mathsf{0}\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathsf{0}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

6.

Secondary Hypothesis

(a)

Right introduction of tensor product (first case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}))\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\end{array}

(b)

Right introduction of tensor product (second case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to previous case.

(c)

Left introduction of tensor product:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\end{array}

(d)

Left introduction of tensor product: second case

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the previous case.

(e)

Left introduction of the unit of tensor:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\end{array}

Contraction

((\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

for any

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})+(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})(\mathit{k})=(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})(\mathit{k})+(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})(\mathit{k})=\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{k})+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{k})\\

(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}})*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}=(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]\\
(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}})*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\end{array}

So we construct the proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi^{\prime}

Lemma 24

(Cut Reduction GS/MS)
.

If
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is a proof of
\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is a proof of
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

and
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
,
then there exists a proof
\Pi
of the sequent
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
.

Proof.

This is by induction on
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.

1.

Commuting Conversions

(a)

left-side:
Suppose we have

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{m}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{m}}]),(\delta\boxast[{(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{m}}]),(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})}^{\mathit{m}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the mutual induction hypothesis
from Lemma
9
(1)
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

and
(\gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\gamma
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]),(\delta\boxast[{\gamma}^{\mathit{n}}]),(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

The rest of the proof is the same as the corresponding
case for Lemma
9
(1)

(b)

cut vs. right-side cut (left case):
Suppose we have:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{m}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},(\delta^{\prime}\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}}^{\mathit{m}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{m}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

and
(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\gamma
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\gamma,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},(\delta^{\prime}\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}}^{\mathit{m}}]),\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\gamma\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\end{array}

This implies:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

Thus, we obtain our result:

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

(c)

cut vs. right-side cut (right case):
Suppose we have:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{5}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{5}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the previous case.

2.

Axiom Cases

(a)

Axiom on the left:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

We know:

\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is a proof of
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}

and that
\mathit{r}*1\leq\mathit{r}

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

3.

Principle Formula vs Principle Formula

(a)

Lin:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_R${}$, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},1)\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know the following:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})=(\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+1)+(\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})+1)\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A})\end{array}

These imply that:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A})\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A})\end{array}

Thus, we apply the induction hypothesis of Lemma
9
(3)
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}
to obtain a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of the sequent

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}((\mathit{A}))
.
Since
(1\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(1*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, we construct
\Pi\,=\,\Pi^{\prime}
.

(b)

Graded Tensor

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{5}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}))\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\\
\mathit{r}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})=(\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

Instead of applying the induction hypothesis,
we can directly build the proof
\Pi
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}\quad\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi_{5}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gcut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gcut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

So
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})

4.

Secondary Conclusion

(a)

Left Introduction of Tensor:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Z}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Z})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Z})
and
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})

where
|\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}|
and
|\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}|
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Z})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

Implies

\begin{array}[]{lll}(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r^{\prime}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

(b)

Left Introduction of Tensor Unit:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})

where
|\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}|
and
|\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}|
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},(\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}]),\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}-\mathsf{MS}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}]\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta\boxast[{\mathit{r}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

(c)

Weakening:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{weak${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})

where
|\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}|
and
|\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}|=|\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}|
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{weak${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\\
\delta\boxast[{\mathsf{0}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\mathsf{0}\\
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\mathsf{0}}^{\mathit{n}}],\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\\
\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})}^{\mathit{n}}]=(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{0},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})\end{array}

5.

Secondary Hypothesis

(a)

Right introduction of tensor product (first case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
(\delta\boxast[{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}))\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\end{array}

(b)

Right introduction of tensor product (second case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to previous case.

(c)

Left introduction of linear tensor product:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}

\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\

:
