(h)

Right introduction of linear implication:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{GR}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\

\Pi
:

Given the above we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\end{array}

(i)

Left introduction of linear implication:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{GL}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}

\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{GL}${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

(j)

Right introduction of Grd:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

We know the following:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+(\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+1)\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

These imply that:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

Thus, we apply the induction hypothesis of Lemma
9
(1) to

\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
to obtain a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of the sequent

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}((\mathit{X}))

and
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Now we define the proof
\Pi
as follows:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi:$}}{(\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$, left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}

with:
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

(k)

Weakening:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{weak${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the unit of the graded tensor case.

(l)

Weakening: second case

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{weak${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the unit of the graded tensor case.

6.

Structural

(a)

Weakening

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{weak${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the structural weakening case for GS

(b)

Contraction

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cont${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the structural contraction case for GS

(c)

Exchange

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
(\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}])=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\\
\delta\boxast[{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}^{\mathit{n}}]=\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\end{array}

(d)

Approximation

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad{\delta^{\prime}\leq\delta}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{sub${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the case for GS

∎

Lemma 25

(Cut Reduction MS)
.

If
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is a proof of
\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}

and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is a proof of
\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}

with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

and
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
,
then there exists a proof
\Pi
of the sequent

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}

with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.

Proof.

This is by induction on
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.

1.

Commuting Conversions

(a)

left-side:
Suppose we have

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\\
\end{array}

This implies:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

Thus, we obtain our result

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(b)

cut vs. right-side cut (left case):
Suppose we have:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\\
\end{array}

This implies:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

Thus, we obtain our result:

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(c)

cut vs. right-side cut (right case):
Suppose we have:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

This is similar to the previous case.

2.

eta-Expansion

(a)

Tensor:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

Since
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,0
we find ourselves in a base
case.
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
are cut-free, so we must produce a
cut-free proof,
\Pi
with which we do as follows:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A},\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$}}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$}

(b)

Tensor Unit:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathsf{I}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{I}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathsf{I}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{I}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

Since
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,0
we find ourselves in a base
case.
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
are cut-free, so we must produce a
cut-free proof,
\Pi
with which we do as follows:

\frac{\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{I}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_R${}$}}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathsf{I}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{I}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_L${}$}

(c)

Grd:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

Since
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,0
we find ourselves in a base
case.
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
are cut-free, so we must produce a
cut-free proof,
\Pi
, which we do as follows:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{\mathit{r}*1\odot\mathit{X};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$}}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_L${}$}

3.

Axiom Cases

(a)

Axiom on the left:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

We know:

\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is a proof of
\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\emptyset\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\emptyset;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

(b)

Axiom on the right:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

We know:

\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is a proof of
\emptyset,\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\emptyset,\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

4.

Principle Formula vs Principle Formula

(a)

Linear Tensor:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\frac{\pi_{5},}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}\end{array}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}))\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})\\
\end{array}

Instead of applying the induction hypothesis,
we can directly build the proof
\Pi
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}\quad\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi_{5}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

So
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{Y})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})

(b)

Tensor Unit:

\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{I}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{I})\\
\end{array}

So
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
is a proof of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\emptyset\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\emptyset;\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\emptyset,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{I})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi\,=\,\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

(c)

Grd:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{4}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X},\Gamma)\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_L${}$, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know the following:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})=(\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+1)+(\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})+1)\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X})\end{array}

These imply that:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X})\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X})\end{array}

Thus, we apply the induction hypothesis of Lemma
9
(2)
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
to obtain a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of the sequent

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X})
.
Which is exactly what we want.

(d)

Linear Implication:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{R}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{5}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}))\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\\
\end{array}

Instead of applying the induction hypothesis,
we can directly build the proof
\Pi
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}\hskip 7.3612pt\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}\hskip 7.3612pt\frac{\pi_{5}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}

So
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{5}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})+1,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})+1)\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})

5.

Secondary Conclusion

(a)

Left Introduction of Linear Tensor:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3},}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{C})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{C})
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{C})\\
\end{array}

(b)

Left Introduction of Linear Tensor Unit:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

We know
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(c)

Left introduction of graded tensor product:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and

implies that there is a proof

of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(d)

Left Introduction of Graded Tensor Unit:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}-\mathsf{MS}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}-\mathsf{MS}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(e)

Left Introduction of Linear Implication:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{C})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{C})

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

We know
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{C})

(f)

Left introduction of Lin:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};(\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},1)\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know the following:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})=(\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+1)+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\end{array}

These imply that:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\end{array}

Thus, we apply the induction hypothesis of Lemma
9
(3) to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
to obtain a proof

\Pi^{\prime}
of the sequent
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})
.
Now we define the proof
\Pi
as follows:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$, left=}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},1)\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$, left=$\Pi:$}

with:
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})

(g)

Left introduction of Grd:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\pi_{2}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know the following:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})=(\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+1)+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\end{array}

These imply that:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})\end{array}

Thus, we apply the induction hypothesis of Lemma
9
(3) to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
to obtain a proof

\Pi^{\prime}
of the sequent
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})
.
Now we define the proof
\Pi
as follows:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$, left=}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$, left=$\Pi:$}

with:
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{B})

6.

Secondary Hypothesis

(a)

Right introduction of linear tensor product (first case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}\otimes\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}\otimes\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$,left=}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}\otimes\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

(b)

Right introduction of linear tensor product (second case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}\otimes\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the previous case.

(c)

Left introduction of linear tensor product:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B}\otimes\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B}\otimes\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

(d)

Left introduction of linear tensor product (second case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B}\otimes\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\otimes_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to previous case.

(e)

Left introduction of the unit of linear tensor:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

(f)

Left introduction of the unit of linear tensor (second case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

Similar to the previous case.

(g)

Left introduction of graded tensor product:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})

Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y}\boxtimes\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

Given the above, we know:

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(h)

Left introduction of the unit of graded tensor:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}-\mathsf{MS}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}-\mathsf{MS}}_L${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

We know
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(i)

Right introduction of linear implication:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}\multimap\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{R}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{B}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}\multimap\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{R}${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

We know
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(j)

Left introduction of linear implication:

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B}\multimap\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B}\multimap\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=$\Pi:$}

We know that
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}))\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(k)

Left introduction of linear implication (second case):

\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{4}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B}\multimap\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}

We know:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\end{array}

and so applying the induction hypothesis
to
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{4}}}

implies that there is a proof
\Pi^{\prime}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}
with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})
.
Thus, we construct the following proof
\Pi
:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\pi_{3}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{B}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}:$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B}\multimap\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\multimap_{L}${}$,left=}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{B}\multimap\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{D}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$,left=$\Pi:$}

We know that
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathbf{Max}\,(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{3}}}))\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{A})

(l)

Left introduction of linear implication (third case):

Similar to the previous case.

.
