∎

A notable example:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\ }{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$, left=}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$, left=}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_R${}$, left=}}{1*1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}_{1}\,\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$, left=}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathsf{Grd}_{1}\,\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}&&\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\ }{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$, left=}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$, left=}}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathsf{Grd}_{1}\,\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Grd}_L${}$, left=}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathsf{Grd}_{1}\,\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathsf{Grd}_{1}\,\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_R${}$,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{0}}}:$}\end{array}

\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{0}}}
would be handled by a secondary hypothesis case for the
right introduction of
\mathsf{Lin}
. The inductive step of the reduction would be on

\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
. The right and left rules for the modal operators line up,
resulting in three principal versus principal cases. Finally the reduction bottoms
out at an axiom case. Resulting in the following proof:

\frac{\frac{\frac{\ }{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$, left=}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$, left=}}{1\odot\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\mathsf{Lin}_R${}$, left=$\Pi^{\prime}:$}

C.2
Decreasing Order of
\mathsf{mGL}{}

See
10

Decreasing order is done termless for simplicity.

Proof.

By induction on
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi)
. For convenience we will only consider
\Pi
a proof of
\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
.
If the last inference in
\Pi
is not a cut, then we simply apply
the induction hypothesis. Thus, suppose the last inference in
\Pi
is a cut on a formula
X
. If

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,>\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1
, then
X
is not the main cut formula in
\Pi
, and hence, we can just
apply the induction hypothesis to the premises of cut. This leaves the case where
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1
, and so
X
is the main cut formula in
\Pi
. This implies
\Pi
is of the following form:

\frac{\frac{\pi_{1}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}\quad\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}^{\mathit{n}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=GS-MCut,left=$\Pi:$}

We assumed above that
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1
, and hence, these imply:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi)\,=\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})+1\\
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi)\\
\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,<\,\mathsf{Depth}(\Pi)\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)=Max(\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}),\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}),\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1)\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1\\
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1\end{array}

Now without loss of generality we assume neither
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
nor
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
are axioms which follow
similarly. By the induction hypothesis we may conclude that there are proofs
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}

and
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
of the same sequents of
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, but with

\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
and
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})
.
Therefore, by applying the cut reduction lemma to
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
we obtain a proof
\Pi^{\prime}

of the sequent
(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Y}
with
\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi^{\prime})\,\leq\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})\,<\,\mathsf{Rank}(\mathit{X})+1\,=\,\mathsf{CutRank}\,(\Pi)
.

∎

C.3
Proof of subformula property

Subformula definitions and property proof are done termless for simplicity.

Definition 26
.

We will define the function
Sf
from formulas to sets of formulas as follows

\begin{array}[]{lll}Sf(\phi)&=&\{\phi\}\text{when }\phi\text{ is atomic}\\
Sf(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})&=&\bigcup\{Sf(X),Sf(Y),\{\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}\}\}\\
Sf(\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B})&=&\bigcup\{Sf(A),Sf(B),\{\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}\}\}\\
Sf(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})&=&\bigcup\{Sf(A),Sf(B),\{\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}\}\}\\
Sf(\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A})&=&Sf(A)\cup\{\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\}\\
Sf(\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X})&=&Sf(X)\cup\{\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}\}\end{array}

Note: if
\phi\in Sf(\psi)
then
Sf(\phi)\subseteq Sf(\psi)

Definition 27
.

We will define
\mathcal{F}(S)
, where
S
is a well formed
judgement, as the set of formulas that appear in the judgement
S
.
For a judgement
\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}
,
\mathcal{F}(\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X})=\{X\}\cup\Delta

where
\Delta
is viewed as a set.

Similarly, we will define
\mathcal{F}[\Pi]
, where
\Pi
is a proof of a judgement

S
as
\mathcal{F}[\Pi]=\mathcal{F}(S)\cup(\bigcup\mathcal{F}[\Pi_{i}])
where

\Pi_{i}
’s are the premises of
\Pi

See
12

With the above definitions we can now restate the subformula property formally as follows:

For any judgement
\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}
and cut-free proof
\Pi
of
\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X}
,

\mathcal{F}[\Pi]\subseteq\bigcup_{\phi\in\mathcal{F}(\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{X})}Sf(\phi)

For any judgement
\delta\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
and cut-free proof
\Pi
of
\delta\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A}
,

\mathcal{F}[\Pi]\subseteq\bigcup_{\phi\in\mathcal{F}(\delta\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{A})}Sf(\phi)

Proof is by mutual induction on derivations.

Proof.

The axiom cases vacuously true. Mutual induction is used in the right
introductions for the Grd and Lin constructors. All the inductive cases
follow a similar pattern. The proof for GS-TenL is provided as an example.

\frac{\frac{\pi_{2}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}:$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=, left=$\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}:$}

By assumption we know

\mathcal{F}[\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}]\subseteq\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\}

We also know

\begin{array}[]{lll}\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\}\\
=\left(\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\{\delta_{1},r,\delta_{2},\Delta_{1},\Delta_{2},Z)\}\right)\cup(\bigcup\{Sf(X),Sf(Y),\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}\})\\
=\left(\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\}\right)\cup\{\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}\}\par\end{array}

So

\mathcal{F}[\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}]\subseteq\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\}

from the definition of
\mathcal{F}[\Pi]

\mathcal{F}[\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}]=\mathcal{F}[\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}]\cup\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})

from the definition of
Sf(\phi)

\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\subseteq\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\}

So we know

\mathcal{F}[\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}]\cup\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\subseteq\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\}

That is,

\mathcal{F}[\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}]\subseteq\bigcup\{Sf(\phi)\mid\phi\in\mathcal{F}((\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{Z})\}

∎

C.4
Denotational model

Definition 28

(Interpretation of Mixed Graded/Linear Logic.)
.

Given a Mixed Graded/Linear model with symmetric monoidal
adjunction
\mathcal{C}:\mathsf{Mny}\dashv\mathsf{Lin}:\mathcal{M}

and strict exponential action

\odot:{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathsf{op}}\times\mathcal{C}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathcal{C}
,
we interpret
\mathsf{mGL}{}
proofs via two mutually
defined interpretations
\llbracket-\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}
and
\llbracket-\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}{}}

on types and proofs (derivations):

•

For every type
X
there is an object
\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}\in\mathcal{C}
and
for every type
A
there is an object
\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\in\mathcal{M}
, mutually defined:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}\llbracket\mathsf{J}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}&=\mathsf{J}\\
\llbracket\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}&=\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}\boxtimes\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}\\
\llbracket\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}&=\mathsf{Lin}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
&\end{array}\quad

\displaystyle\quad\begin{array}[]{rl}\llbracket\mathsf{I}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}&=I\\
\llbracket\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}&=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\llbracket\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}&=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\multimap\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\llbracket\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}&=\mathsf{Mny}(r\odot\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\end{array}

•

\mathsf{GS}{}
contexts are interpetered as objects composed of tensors, e.g.,
for the denotation of a derivation
\Pi
of

(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})\odot(\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{x_{\mathit{n}}}:\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}

its source is given by:

\displaystyle\emptyset^{\mathsf{GS}}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{J}

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{x_{\mathit{n}}}:\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})}^{\mathsf{GS}}

\displaystyle=(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})

•

\mathsf{MS}{}
contexts are interpetered as object composed of tensors, e.g.,
for the denotation of a derivation of

(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})\odot(\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{x_{\mathit{n}}}:\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}});\mathit{y_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{y_{\mathit{m}}}:\mathit{A_{\mathit{m}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}

its source is given by:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\llbracket(\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{x_{\mathit{n}}}:\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}});(\mathit{y_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{y_{\mathit{m}}}:\mathit{A_{\mathit{m}}})\rrbracket_{(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\,\,\,\,\,=\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\llbracket\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes\ldots\otimes\llbracket\mathit{A_{\mathit{m}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

•

The interpretation of proofs is given as follows by induction
on the structure of proofs, considering each proof rule in turn (see below).

Notational conventions

We assume below
that the premise proofs of each rule are called
\Pi

if there is only one, or are numbered
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, etc.
if there are more than one.

For brevity we often ommit the interpretation brackets
\llbracket-\rrbracket
.

For
\delta=r_{1},\ldots,r_{n}

and
\Delta=X_{1},\ldots,X_{n}

\delta{\odot}\Delta=(r_{1}{\odot}X_{1})\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes(r_{n}{\odot}X_{n})
.

To apply an operation
\mathit{op}
to a
n
-ary product, we write
\overline{\mathit{op}}
.

Derived operations

As per Proposition 1 of Benton
[
3
]
, an adjunction over monoidal categories,
whose functors are lax monoidal, induces a strong monoidal structure. We use this on the
left, where:

\displaystyle n_{\boxtimes}^{\mathsf{Mny}}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Mny}(A\boxtimes B)

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathsf{Mny}(\eta\boxtimes\eta)}\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{Lin}(\mathsf{Mny}A)\boxtimes\mathsf{Lin}(\mathsf{Mny}B))

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathsf{Mny}(m^{\mathsf{Lin}})}\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{Lin}(\mathsf{Mny}A\otimes\mathsf{Mny}B))

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\varepsilon}\mathsf{Mny}A\otimes\mathsf{Mny}B

and

\displaystyle n_{\mathsf{J}}^{\mathsf{Mny}}:\mathsf{Mny}\mathsf{J}\xrightarrow{\mathsf{Mny}(m^{\mathsf{Lin}}_{\mathsf{I}})}\mathsf{Mny}\mathsf{Lin}\mathsf{J}\xrightarrow{\varepsilon}\mathsf{I}

For a (lax/strong) monoidal functor, we can derive a
n
-ary version of its monoidal
operation in a standard way.
For example, for
\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}:\mathsf{Mny}A\otimes\mathsf{Mny}B\rightarrow\mathsf{Mny}(A\boxtimes B)
, we write its
n
-ary version as

\displaystyle\overline{m^{\mathsf{Mny}}}:\mathsf{Mny}A_{1}\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes\mathsf{Mny}A_{n}\rightarrow\mathsf{Mny}(A_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes A_{n})

where for
0
-arity we let

\displaystyle m^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{0}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{J}\rightarrow\mathsf{Mny}\mathsf{I}

\displaystyle m^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{0}

\displaystyle=m_{\mathsf{J}}

and for
n+1
then (where we write
\overline{\Delta}_{n}
for the
n
-times product of
objects in
\Delta
to which we can lift functors pointwise):

\displaystyle m^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Mny}A\boxtimes\overline{\mathsf{Mny}\Delta}_{n}\rightarrow\mathsf{Mny}(A\otimes\overline{\Delta}_{n})

\displaystyle m^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle=m^{\mathsf{Mny}}\circ(id\boxtimes m^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{n})

We will work up to associativity (which is witnessed by an isomorphism).

Inductive definition - GS

–

–

(
id
GS
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{}{1\odot\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket=1\odot\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46605pt\raise 6.47223pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-3.47223pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\text{\normalsize{$\mathsf{id}$}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.24382pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 22.48764pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket

the soundness of which follows by the following property:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rrl}&1\odot\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}\\
\equiv&\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}&\{\textit{strict action}\}\end{array}

–

(
\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_{R}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{j}:\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_{R}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

We need a morphism:

\displaystyle{\llbracket\emptyset\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.44604pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\mathit{j}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.2905pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.581pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathsf{J}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}

\displaystyle\equiv\;\;

\displaystyle\mathsf{J}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.4479pt\raise 5.43056pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.43056pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{id}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 7.94398pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.88797pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathsf{J}

–

(
\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_{L}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{j}\,=\mathit{x}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_{L}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By induction we have the following morphism:

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48259pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.98956pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 37.97913pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}

Then the final interpretation is as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes(\mathit{r}\odot\mathsf{J})\boxtimes(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 7.79916pt\raise 6.62083pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.58752pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{id\boxtimes\,n_{\mathsf{J},r}\,\boxtimes id}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 29.3822pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 64.7644pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes\mathsf{J}\boxtimes(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.69711pt\raise 5.60417pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.60417pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\lambda\boxtimes id}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.68973pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 37.37946pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48259pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 15.98956pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 37.97913pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}\end{array}

–

(
\boxtimes_{R}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}(\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\boxtimes_{R}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket=

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{l}:\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Lin}_{R}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket=

First, by induction we have the interpretation of the premise as follows:

\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket

where
\delta\odot\Delta=(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})\odot(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}})

\begin{array}[]{rll}&((\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}))\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes((\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}))\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.4955pt\raise 7.75pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.25pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\text{\normalsize{$\eta_{r_{1}}\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes\eta_{r_{n}}$}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 30.93135pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 67.8627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Lin}(\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}))\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes\mathsf{Lin}(\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket))\\

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By induction we have the following morphisms:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\delta_{2}\odot\Delta_{2}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48749pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.6403pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 35.28061pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathit{X}\\
\delta_{1}\odot\Delta_{1}\boxtimes r\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{3}\odot\Delta_{3}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48749pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.6403pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 35.28061pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathit{X}\\

First, we promote
\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket
:

\begin{array}[]{lll}&r\odot(\delta_{2}\odot\Delta_{2})\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.69434pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{r\odot\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.9363pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 49.87259pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>r\odot\mathit{X}\\
\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\delta_{\Delta_{2},r,\delta_{2}}}}{{=}}&(r*\delta_{2})\odot\Delta_{2}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.69434pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{r\odot\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.9363pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 49.87259pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>r\odot\mathit{X}\end{array}

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Y}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathsf{0},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{weak${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\delta_{1}\odot\Delta_{1}\boxtimes 0\odot\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}\boxtimes\delta_{2}\odot\Delta_{2}\\
