\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Y}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{x}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{cont${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By induction we have a morphism:

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\boxtimes\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48997pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.69138pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 31.38275pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathit{Y}

The final interpretation is therefore as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.71733pt\raise 6.61389pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.63612pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\boxtimes(\mathsf{contr}_{r1,r2,X})\boxtimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 41.12575pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 88.2515pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\odot\mathit{X})\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.87398pt\raise 4.34428pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.34428pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{=}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.94344pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.88687pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\boxtimes\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48997pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.69138pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 31.38275pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathit{Y}\par\end{array}

–

(
ex
GS
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{ex${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

This case easily follows from symmetry of
\boxtimes
the tensor product in
[\mathcal{R},\mathcal{C}]^{\odot}
.

–

(
sub
GS
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{sub${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

This interpretation is constructed via functoriality of
\odot
in its first argument:

\displaystyle\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\llbracket\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\odot\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket

Note the syntactic sugar on rules for (sub) given at the start
of Section
A.1
allows a refinement of the
inequality to focus on just one grade in the context. That is,
given an inequality
\mathit{r}\leq\mathit{s}
then we write:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X^{\prime}},\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Y}\quad\mathit{r}\leq\mathit{s}}{\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{X^{\prime}},\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{right=$\textsc{sub${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

In this case, we can specialise the semantics to:

\displaystyle(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket)\boxtimes(s\odot\llbracket\mathit{X^{\prime}}\rrbracket)\boxtimes(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket)\xrightarrow{id\boxtimes(\llbracket\mathit{s}\leq\mathit{r}\rrbracket\odot\llbracket X^{\prime}\rrbracket)\boxtimes id}(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket)\boxtimes(r\odot\llbracket\mathit{X^{\prime}}\rrbracket)\boxtimes(\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket)\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket

•

Inductive definition - MS

The interpretation of proofs is given as follows by induction
on the structure of proofs, considering each proof rule in turn.
We assume below
that the premise proofs of each rule are called
\Pi

if there is only one, or are numbered
\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, etc.
if there are more than one.

–

(
id
MS
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{id${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

The final interpretation is as follows:

{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.45001pt\raise 5.43056pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.43056pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 7.09445pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 20.1889pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

–

(
sub
MS
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{sub${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

The assumption
\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}
corresponds to a list of morphism
in
\mathcal{R}
. Thus, this case follows from functorality in the
first argument of
\mathsf{Mny}
.

–

(
\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_{R}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{i}:\mathsf{I}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_{R}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

We need a morphism:

I\otimes I\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.4749pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\mathit{i}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.8402pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 35.68039pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>I

Thus we simply choose
\lambda_{I}
of the monoidal category
\mathcal{M}
.

–

(
\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_{L}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}}{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathsf{I},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{i}\,=\mathit{x}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{I}}_{L}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By induction we have the following morphism:

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

Then the final interpretation is as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes I\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.87398pt\raise 4.34428pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.34428pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{=}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.94344pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.88687pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes({\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes I)\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.67659pt\raise 5.84723pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.01389pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes\lambda\otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 20.58704pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 47.17407pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

–

(
\multimap_{R}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{\delta\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\multimap_{R}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

We know by assumption that
\mathcal{M}
is closed, and thus, there is a natural isomoprhism:

\mathsf{Hom}_{\mathcal{C}}(C\otimes A,B)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.47238pt\raise 5.2361pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-0.875pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{curry}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 11.84184pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 29.68369pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathsf{Hom}_{\mathcal{C}}(C,\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})

Now by induction we have the following morphism:

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

The final interpretation is as follows:

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46124pt\raise 7.12001pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.37001pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{curry}({\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 26.98352pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 59.96704pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>({\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\multimap{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})

–

(
\multimap_{L}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{C}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathit{z}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\multimap_{L}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

Recall that due to
\mathcal{M}
being closed we have the following natural isomorphism:

\mathsf{Hom}_{\mathcal{C}}(C\otimes A,B)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.47238pt\raise 5.2361pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-0.875pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{curry}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 11.84184pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 29.68369pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathsf{Hom}_{\mathcal{C}}(C,\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})

Now replacing
C
with
\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B}
we obtain the following:

\mathsf{Hom}_{\mathcal{C}}((\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\otimes A,B)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.47238pt\raise 5.2361pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-0.875pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{curry}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 11.84184pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 29.68369pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathsf{Hom}_{\mathcal{C}}(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B},\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})

Thus,
\mathsf{curry}^{-1}(\mathsf{id}):(\mathit{A}\multimap\mathit{B})\otimes\mathit{A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>B
.

Next by the induction hypothesis we have the following morphisms:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.459pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.43822pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.87643pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.459pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.43822pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.87643pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{C}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

First, we can compose
{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}
and
\mathsf{curry}^{-1}(\mathsf{id})
:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes({\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\multimap{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.87398pt\raise 4.34428pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.34428pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{=}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.94344pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.88687pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&({\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\multimap{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.71153pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{id\otimes\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.48544pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 52.97089pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&({\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\multimap{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})\otimes{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46605pt\raise 6.99501pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.24501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{curry}^{-1}(\mathsf{id})}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 20.73695pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 47.4739pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\end{array}

We will denote the previous composition by
{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}=(\mathsf{curry}^{-1}(\mathsf{id}))\circ(id\otimes\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket)
in the final interpretation:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes({\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\multimap{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.87398pt\raise 4.34428pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.34428pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{=}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.94344pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.88687pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes({\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\multimap{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46623pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 28.03294pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 62.06589pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.459pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.43822pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.87643pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket\mathit{C}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

–

(
\otimes_{L}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{A},\mathit{y}:\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{let}\,(\mathit{x},\mathit{y})=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\otimes_{L}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

This case follows directly from the induction hypothesis.

–

(
\otimes_{R}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}(\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{A}\otimes\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\otimes_{R}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By the induction hypothesis we have the following morphisms:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.459pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.43822pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.87643pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.459pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.43822pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.87643pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\end{array}

The final interpretation is as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.87398pt\raise 4.34428pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.34428pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{=}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.94344pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.88687pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49535pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 35.5788pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 77.1576pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

–

(
\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}-\mathsf{MS}}_{L}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{j}\,=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}-\mathsf{MS}}_{L}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By the induction hypothesis we have:

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

The interpretation is then:

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(r\odot J)\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.56438pt\raise 6.61389pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.63612pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(n_{J,r})\otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.02965pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 74.0593pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(J)\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.59238pt\raise 6.74779pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.74223pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes n^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{J}\otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 24.4849pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 54.96979pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes I\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.87398pt\raise 4.34428pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.34428pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{=}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.94344pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.88687pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

–

(
\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{let}\,(\mathit{x},\mathit{y})=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\boxtimes_{L-\mathsf{MS}}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By the induction hypothesis we have the following:

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

The final interpretation is then:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot({\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}\boxtimes{\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}))\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.57843pt\raise 7.50485pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.84633pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(n^{\odot}_{\boxtimes,r})\otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 34.92915pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 75.8583pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}((\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes(\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}))\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.59238pt\raise 7.08817pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.40186pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes n^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{\boxtimes}\otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 24.4849pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 54.96979pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

–

(
\mathsf{Grd}_{R}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}}{\mathit{r}*\delta\odot\Delta;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{t}:\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Grd}_{R}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

Suppose
\delta\odot\Delta
=
(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})\odot(\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{x_{\mathit{n}}}:\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}})
.
Then by the inductive hypothesis we have:

(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48997pt\raise 6.15pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.69138pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 31.38275pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}

The final interpretation is as follows:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}((\mathit{r}*\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathsf{Mny}((\mathit{r}*\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\\
\xrightarrow{\overline{m^{\mathsf{Mny}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(((\mathit{r}*\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}\boxtimes\cdots\boxtimes((\mathit{r}*\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}})\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}))\\
=^{\mathsf{Mny}(\delta^{\odot}\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes\delta^{\odot})}&\mathsf{Mny}((\mathit{r}\odot(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}}))\boxtimes\cdots\boxtimes(\mathit{r}\odot(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})))\\
\xrightarrow{\mathsf{Mny}(\overline{m_{\boxtimes,r,X,Y}})}&\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot((\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes\cdots\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket^{\mathsf{GS}})))\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.6818pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{Mny}(r\odot\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket)}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 29.58209pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 65.16418pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(r\odot\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket)^{\mathsf{GS}}\end{array}

–

(
\mathsf{Lin}_{L}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;(\mathit{x}:\mathit{A},\Gamma)\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta,1)\odot(\Delta,\mathit{z}:\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathsf{Unlin}\,\mathit{z}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Lin}_{L}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By induction we have the following morphism:

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket B\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

Thus, the final interpretation is as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rlll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(1\odot\mathsf{Lin}{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
=&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{Lin}{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.56046pt\raise 5.84723pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.01389pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes\ \varepsilon\ \otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.08566pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 52.17133pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&{\llbracket B\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{array}

–

(
\mathsf{Grd}_{L}
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{(\delta,\mathit{r})\odot(\Delta,\mathit{x}:\mathit{X});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}{\delta\odot\Delta;(\mathit{z}:\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X},\Gamma)\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{x}=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Grd}_{L}$}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

By induction we have the following morphism:

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta\odot{\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(r\odot{\llbracket X\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket C\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

Notice that this is exactly the interpretation of the conclusion.

(
cut
MS
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket

This case follows from simply composing the two morphisms obtained from the induction hypothesis.

(
gcut
MS
)

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{gcut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket=

By induction on the premises we have:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}\xrightarrow{{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}\\
&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.459pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.43822pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.87643pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket B\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{aligned}

Let
\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}=r_{1},\ldots,r_{n}

and let
\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\ldots,\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}
.

Then we construct the interpretation:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}*\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}*\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\stackrel{{\scriptstyle id\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta)\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta)\otimes id}}{{=}}\;\;&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}))\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}))\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\xrightarrow{id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id}\;\;&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}((\mathit{r}\odot(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}))\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes(\mathit{r}\odot(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})))\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 3.74217pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-3.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.55554pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}$}}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 1.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-3.74217pt\raise 6.25555pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.42221pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}_{\boxtimes,r}}\otimes id}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{}{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 1.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}}}{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 1.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}}}{\hbox{\kern 27.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.55554pt\hbox{$\textstyle{}$}}}}}}}\ignorespaces}}}}\ignorespaces\;\;&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot((\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\boxtimes\ldots\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{\mathit{n}}}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X_{\mathit{n}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})))\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\xrightarrow{id\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(id_{r}\odot{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes id}\;\;&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathit{r}\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\xrightarrow{{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}&{\llbracket B\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\end{aligned}

This case follows by applying
\mathsf{Mny}(
to the first
morphism obtained from the induction hypothesis, and then
composing it with the second morphism obtained from the
induction hypothesis.

(
weak
MS
)

\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46149pt\raise 6.945pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.54501pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 16.48929pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 38.97858pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\llbracket B\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}

\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(0\odot{\llbracket X\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.58795pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{id}\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{weak})\otimes\mathsf{id}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 33.97968pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 73.95935pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}\mathsf{J}\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\
\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48824pt\raise 6.74779pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.74223pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{n^{\mathsf{Mny}}_{I}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 11.84184pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 29.68369pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{I}\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}&\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}((\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\odot{\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot{\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}})\otimes{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\\

\displaystyle\Biggl\llbracket\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{A},\mathit{y}:\mathit{B},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}{\delta\odot\Delta;(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{B},\mathit{x}:\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{C}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={{ex${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}}{},}\Biggr\rrbracket
