C.5
Proof of soundness of Mixed Graded/Linear Logic

Soundness proof

See
19

Note that, by the definition of
\equiv
which includes the source- and target of
cut-reductions, then we first proof the following sub-lemma before we prove
the remaining rules of the equational theory not induced by cut-reduction
(Section
C.5.1
):

Lemma 29
.

Suppose
\mathcal{C}:\mathsf{Mny}\dashv\mathsf{Lin}:\mathcal{M}
with
\odot:\mathcal{R}^{op}\times\mathcal{C}\rightarrow\mathcal{C}

a mixed graded/linear model.
Then:

1.

If using the cut-reduction strategy a derivation

\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}

reduces to
the derivation
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{X}
,
then

{\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}:\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket_{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket={\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}:\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket_{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\llbracket\mathit{X}\rrbracket

with
\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

2.

If using the cut-reduction strategy a derivation

\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}

reduces to the derivation
\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
of

\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta;\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
, then

{\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}:\llbracket\Delta;\Gamma\rrbracket_{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket=\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket:\llbracket\Delta;\Gamma\rrbracket_{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket

with
\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Proof.

This is a proof by mutual induction on the form of the cut-reduction
strategy.

1.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\quad\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta,\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}][\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to the derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta,\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta,\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

This case follows from associativity of composition of morphisms:

\displaystyle(lhs)\;\;

\displaystyle(\llbracket\Pi_{3}\rrbracket\circ(\mathsf{id}_{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\odot\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket)\boxtimes\mathsf{id}_{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\rrbracket}))

\displaystyle\qquad\circ(\mathsf{id}_{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\odot\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket\boxtimes\mathsf{id}_{(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\rrbracket})

\displaystyle(rhs)\;\;

\displaystyle\llbracket\Pi_{3}\rrbracket\circ(\mathsf{id}_{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket}\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\odot(\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket\circ(\mathsf{id}_{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}\boxtimes(\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\odot\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket)\boxtimes\mathsf{id}_{\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket})))\boxtimes\mathsf{id}_{\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\rrbracket})

2.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\quad\frac{\delta^{\prime}\odot\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta^{\prime},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta,\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta^{\prime},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}][\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to the derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{\prime}\odot\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Y}\quad\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta^{\prime},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}*\delta^{\prime},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta,\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta^{\prime},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}][\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

This case follows from associativity of composition of morphisms (similarly to the above).

3.

The derivation:

\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{x}:(\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

expands to the derivation:

\frac{\frac{\frac{}{1\odot\mathit{y}:\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{y}:\mathit{X}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{}{1\odot\mathit{z}:\mathit{Y}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{z}:\mathit{Y}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{(1,1)\odot(\mathit{y}:\mathit{X},\mathit{z}:\mathit{Y})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}(\mathit{y},\mathit{z}):\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$}}{(1)\odot(\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{let}\,(\mathit{y},\mathit{z})=\mathit{x}\,\mathsf{in}\,(\mathit{y},\mathit{z}):\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$}

This case follows from the fact that
\odot
is a bifunctor
and that
n_{\boxtimes,r,X,Y}
is strict, such that:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rll}(rhs)&&\;\;(id_{{\llbracket X\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}\boxtimes id_{{\llbracket Y\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}})\circ n_{\boxtimes,1,{\llbracket X\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}},{\llbracket Y\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}\\
&=&\;\;(id_{{\llbracket X\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}\boxtimes id_{{\llbracket Y\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}})\\
(lhs)&=&\;\;id_{{{\llbracket X\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}\boxtimes{\llbracket Y\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{GS}}}\\
\end{array}

4.

The derivation:

\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{x}:\mathsf{J}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{x}:\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to the derivation:

\frac{\frac{\,}{\emptyset\odot\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{j}:\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_R${}$}}{1\odot\mathit{x}:\mathsf{J}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{j}\,=\mathit{x}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathsf{j}:\mathsf{J}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\text{unit}^{\mathsf{J}}_L${}$}

The interpretations are then:

\displaystyle(lhs)

\displaystyle=id_{\mathsf{J}}

\displaystyle(rhs)

\displaystyle=id_{\mathsf{J}}\circ\circ n_{\mathsf{J},1}

which are equivalent by the strict monoidality of
n
.

5.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*1,\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{x}/\mathit{y}]\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to:

(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{Z}

This case follows from the identity law of composition of morphisms.

6.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}\quad\frac{\,}{1\odot\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{id${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{1*\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{x}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to:

\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}

This case follows from the identity law of composition of morphisms.

7.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\quad\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta,\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{X}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta,\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}*\delta,\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta,\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}*$}

This case follows from naturality of symmetry, and the fact that
exchange is interpreted as the symmetry in the symmetric
monoidal category
\mathcal{C}
.

8.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\circ(\mathsf{id}_{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}\boxtimes\mathit{r}\odot\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket\boxtimes\mathsf{id}_{(\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\rrbracket})

13.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{z}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{z}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

This case follows from naturality of symmetry, and the fact that
exchange is interpreted as the symmetry in
\mathcal{C}
which is symmetric monoidal.

14.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{w}:\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{let}\,(\mathit{x},\mathit{y})=\mathit{w}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{w}:\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathsf{let}\,(\mathit{x},\mathit{y})=\mathit{w}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X},\mathit{y}:\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{z}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{r},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{w}:\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{let}\,(\mathit{x},\mathit{y})=\mathit{w}\,\mathsf{in}\,[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{z}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

Both have identical interpretations up to symmetry as per Definition
28
and by the strictness of
n_{\boxtimes}
.

15.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},1,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{w}:\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{j}\,=\mathit{w}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_L${}$}\quad(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},1,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{w}:\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{j}\,=\mathit{w}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{Z}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathit{Z},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{z}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},1,\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}),\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{w}:\mathsf{J},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{j}\,=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{z}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{W}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{ex${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

Both have identical interpretations up to symmetry as per Definition
28
.

16.

The derivation:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{W}\quad\frac{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{w}:\mathit{W},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{X}\hskip 8.19447pt\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}:\mathit{Y}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{w}:\mathit{W},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}(\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}):\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$}\quad}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{w}](\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}):\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}

reduces to:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{W}\hskip 8.19447pt(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{w}:\mathit{W},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{X}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}[\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{w}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{GS}}$}{}$}\quad\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}:\mathit{Y}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{s}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{4}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}([\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{w}]\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{2}}}},\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}):\mathit{X}\boxtimes\mathit{Y}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$$\boxtimes_R${}$}

This case holds by functorality of
\boxtimes
. The case where
\mathit{w}:\mathit{W}
is free in
\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{3}}}}
is similar.

Finally, there are a few additional cases for the structural
rules, but all of them are equivalent to naturality of their
respective natural transformations, and hence, we omit them here.

17.

The final cases are the commuting conversions related to the
rule:

\frac{\delta\odot\Delta;\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}{\delta\odot\Delta\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{l}:\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{narrower=0.3,lab={$\mathsf{Lin}_{R}$}{},}

However, are all similar to the previous cases which hold by
straightforwardly applying the induction hypotheses.

We now consider part two. Here we only give the beta-reduction
cases for the modal operators, because they are the most
interesting.

Consider the following reduction:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\frac{\frac{\Pi_{2}\hskip 7.3612pt\hskip 7.3612pt\vdots}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right={ $$\mathsf{Lin}_R${}$}}\hskip 8.19447pt\frac{\frac{\Pi_{1}\hskip 7.3612pt\hskip 7.3612pt\vdots}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\mathit{x}:\mathit{A},\Gamma)\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\hskip 7.3612pt\textsc{flushleft,right=}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},1)\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{A});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathsf{Unlin}\,\mathit{z}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right={{ $$\mathsf{Lin}_L${}$}}}\hskip 8.19447pt}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},1*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{z}][\mathsf{Unlin}\,\mathit{z}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right={ $\textsc{gcut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$},fraction={---}}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathsf{Unlin}\,(\mathsf{Lin}\,\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}})/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=,fraction={===}}

This proof reduces to the following:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\Pi_{2}\hskip 8.19447pt\hskip 8.19447pt\vdots}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=}\frac{\Pi_{1}\hskip 8.19447pt\hskip 8.19447pt\vdots}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\mathit{x}:\mathit{A},\Gamma)\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right={ $\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$},fraction={---}}

The previous reduction corresponds to the following equation in the interpretation (Definition
28
):

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes\varepsilon\otimes id)\circ(id\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(id_{1}\odot(\mathsf{Lin}{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ\mathsf{m}_{\mathsf{Lin}}\circ\overline{\eta}))\otimes id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})\circ(id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id)\\
\{1\odot A\equiv A\}\equiv\;\;&{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes\varepsilon\otimes id)\circ(id\otimes\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{Lin}{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ\mathsf{m}_{\mathsf{Lin}}\circ\overline{\eta})\otimes id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})\circ(id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id)\\
\{\textit{$\varepsilon$ naturality}\}\equiv\;\;&{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes id)\circ(id\otimes(\varepsilon\circ\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{m}_{\mathsf{Lin}}\circ\overline{\eta})\otimes)id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})\circ(id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id)\\
\{\textit{strong monoidal $\mathsf{Mny}$}\}\equiv\;\;&{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes id)\circ(id\otimes(\varepsilon\circ\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{m}_{\mathsf{Lin}}\circ\overline{\eta})\circ\overline{m^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\circ\overline{n^{\mathsf{Mny}}})\otimes)id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})\circ(id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id)\\
\{\textit{$m^{\mathsf{Mny}}$ naturality}\}\equiv\;\;&{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes id)\circ(id\otimes(\varepsilon\circ\mathsf{Mny}(\mathsf{m}_{\mathsf{Lin}})\circ\overline{m^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\circ\overline{\mathsf{Mny}(\eta)}\circ\overline{n^{\mathsf{Mny}}})\otimes)id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})\circ(id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id)\\
\{\textit{monoidal adjunction}\}\equiv\;\;&{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes id)\circ(id\otimes(\overline{\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Mny}}}\circ\overline{\mathsf{Mny}(\eta)}\circ\overline{n^{\mathsf{Mny}}})\otimes)id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})\circ(id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id)\\
\{\textit{adjunction}\}\equiv\;\;&{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes id)\circ(id\otimes\overline{n^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})\circ(id\otimes\overline{\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{Mny}}}\otimes id)\\
\{\textit{strong monoidal $\mathsf{Mny}$}\}\equiv\;\;&{\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\circ(id\otimes{\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket}^{\mathsf{MS}}\otimes id)\end{aligned}

Next we consider the second beta-reduction for the modal operators. The following proof:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{\frac{\frac{\Pi_{2}\hskip 7.3612pt\hskip 7.3612pt\vdots}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=}}{\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}};\emptyset\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{t}:\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right={ $$\mathsf{Grd}_R${}$}}\quad\frac{\frac{\Pi_{1}\hskip 7.3612pt\hskip 7.3612pt\vdots}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\hskip 8.19447pt\textsc{flushleft,right=}}{\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}};(\mathit{z}:\mathsf{Grd}_{\mathit{r}}\,\mathit{X},\Gamma)\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{x}=\mathit{z}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right={$$\mathsf{Grd}_L${}$}}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathsf{let}\,\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{x}=\mathsf{Grd}\,\mathit{r}\,\mathit{t}\,\mathsf{in}\,\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right={ $\textsc{cut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}}\end{aligned}

reduces to the following proof:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{\frac{\Pi_{2}\hskip 8.19447pt\hskip 8.19447pt\vdots}{\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash_{\mathsf{GS}}\mathit{t}:\mathit{X}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=}\quad\frac{\Pi_{1}\hskip 8.19447pt\hskip 8.19447pt\vdots}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:\mathit{X});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=}}{(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot(\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}});\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{MS}}[\mathit{t}/\mathit{x}]\mathit{l}:\mathit{B}}\quad\textsc{flushleft,right=$\textsc{gcut${}_{\mathsf{MS}}$}{}$}\end{aligned}

We then have the following interpretation of the lhs:

\displaystyle\llbracket\Pi_{1}\rrbracket\circ(id\otimes(\mathsf{Mny}(r\odot\llbracket\Pi_{2}\rrbracket)\circ\mathsf{Mny}(\overline{m_{\boxtimes,r,X,Y}})\circ\overline{m^{\mathsf{Mny}}})\otimes id_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket})

which is exactly the interpretation of the rhs, due to the strict action
properties and the strictness of the exponential action.
∎

C.5.1
Soundness of the (rest of the) equational theory

By induction of the definition of the equational theory
\equiv
(Definition
A.1
).

\mathsf{GS}
system

•

(
contr-sym
)

\displaystyle\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\boxtimes(\mathit{r}+\mathit{s})\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket\circ(id\boxtimes(\mathsf{contr}_{r,s,X})\boxtimes id)}\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket

(lhs)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\boxtimes(\mathit{s}+\mathit{r})\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket\circ(id\boxtimes c_{X,X}\boxtimes id)\circ(id\boxtimes\mathsf{contr_{s,r,X}}\boxtimes id)}\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket

(rhs)

by the symmetry of the colax monoidal part of the exponential
action (Definition
16
);

•

(
contr-unitL
)

\displaystyle(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}})\boxtimes(\mathit{r}\odot\llbracket X\rrbracket)\boxtimes(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})

(lhs)

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket\circ(\lambda\boxtimes id)\circ(id\boxtimes\mathsf{weak}_{X}\boxtimes id)\circ(id\boxtimes\mathsf{contr}_{r,0,X}\boxtimes id)}\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}})\boxtimes(\mathit{r}\odot\llbracket X\rrbracket)\boxtimes(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket}\llbracket\mathit{Y}\rrbracket

(rhs)

By the unitality of the colax monoidal action.

Similar to the above by unitality

(
sub-comm-conv
)
Let
f=\llbracket\delta\leq\delta^{\prime}\rrbracket
, then:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}[\delta^{\prime}\odot\Delta]\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Phi\rrbracket(\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket)\circ\mathcal{G}(f)}\llbracket X^{\prime}\rrbracket

(lhs)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}[\delta^{\prime}\odot\Delta]\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Phi\rrbracket(\llbracket\Pi\circ\mathcal{G}(f)\rrbracket)}\llbracket X^{\prime}\rrbracket

(rhs)

by naturality properties of the underlying transformations.

(
contr-assoc
) By the associativity of
\mathsf{contr}
.

(
ex-ex
) By the involutive property of the commutativity
natural isomorphism
c
for
\boxtimes
.

(
sub-refl
) Since
\mathcal{R}
is a thin category
then any interpretation of
\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is the identity morphisms,
and thus this equation is preserved
in the semantics by the functor identity property of
\mathsf{Grd}

in the first argument.

(
sub-trans
) by the functoriality of
\mathsf{Grd}
in the
first argument.

(
contr-mono
) follows by naturality of
\mathsf{contr}

with respect to the input functor
F\ r\ s\ X=(r+s)\odot X

and the output functor
G\ r\ s\ X=(r\odot X)\boxtimes(s\odot X)
.

(
sub-unitl
)

\displaystyle(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket)\boxtimes(\mathit{s}\odot\mathsf{J})\boxtimes(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket)\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket\circ(\lambda\boxtimes id)\circ(id\boxtimes n_{\mathsf{J},r}\boxtimes id)\circ(id\boxtimes(\llbracket\mathit{r}\leq\mathit{s}\rrbracket\odot\mathsf{J})\boxtimes id)}\llbracket X\rrbracket

(lhs)

\displaystyle\equiv\;

\displaystyle(\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket)\boxtimes(\mathit{s}\odot\mathsf{J})\boxtimes(\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket)\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi\rrbracket\circ(\lambda\boxtimes id)\circ(id\boxtimes n_{\mathsf{J},s}\boxtimes id)}\llbracket X\rrbracket

(rhs)

where
n_{\mathsf{J},r}\circ(\llbracket\mathit{r}\leq\mathit{s}\rrbracket\odot\mathsf{J})\equiv n_{\mathsf{J},s}
follows by the terminality of
\mathsf{J}
/ or alternatively via naturality of
n_{\mathsf{J}}
in
the first parameter.

(
sub-tensorL
) follows by naturality of
n_{\boxtimes}
in the first parameter (grade parameter).

(
mult-mono
)

\displaystyle\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes(\mathit{r^{\prime}}*\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{id\boxtimes(r^{\prime}\odot(\llbracket\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\odot\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket))\boxtimes id}\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes\mathit{r^{\prime}}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{id\boxtimes(\llbracket\mathit{r}\leq\mathit{r^{\prime}}\rrbracket\odot X)\boxtimes id}\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes\mathit{r}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}Y

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes(\mathit{r^{\prime}}*\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{id\boxtimes(\llbracket\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\mathit{r^{\prime}}*\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\odot\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\boxtimes id}\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes(\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}})\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{id\boxtimes(r\odot\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket)\boxtimes id}\delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket\boxtimes\mathit{r}\odot\mathit{X}\boxtimes\delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\odot\llbracket\Delta_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket}Y

by functoriality of
\odot
in both arguments, and its definition, such that:

\displaystyle(\llbracket\mathit{r}\leq\mathit{r^{\prime}}\rrbracket\odot id_{X})\circ(id_{r^{\prime}}\odot(\llbracket\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\odot\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket))

\displaystyle\equiv\;

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{r}\leq\mathit{r^{\prime}}\rrbracket\odot(\llbracket\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\odot\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket))

\displaystyle\equiv\;

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\mathit{r^{\prime}}*\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\odot\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\equiv\;

\displaystyle(id_{r}\odot\llbracket\Pi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket)\circ(\llbracket\mathit{r}*\delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\mathit{r^{\prime}}*\delta^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket\odot id_{\Delta_{{\mathrm{2}}}})

\mathsf{MS}{}
system
